But looping over lags and assigning slices. For K up to 100, okay.
Pseudo:
def weighted_gradient(h, lags, weights):
    n=len(h); v=np.zeros(n)
    for s, w in zip(lags, weights):
        if w==0: continue
        # first range
        lo_f = max(0, s - (n-1)); hi_f = min(n-1, s)
        if lo_f <= hi_f:
            p = np.arange(lo_f, hi_f+1)
            j = p - s + (n-1)
            v[p] += w * (1.0 - h[j])
        lo_b = max(0, s); hi_b = min(n-1, s + n -1)
        if lo_b <= hi_b:
            p = np.arange(lo_b, hi_b+1)
            j = p - s + (n-1)
            v[p] -= w * h[j]
    return v
This uses numpy arange per lag; overhead. Could precompute for each s? But K small.

Let's implement straightforward but optimized:
def weighted_topq_gradient(h, lags):
    n=len(h)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    scale = 1.0/len(lags)
    base = n-1
    for s in lags:
        lo = s - base
        if lo < 0: lo = 0
        hi = s
        if hi >= n: hi = n-1
        if lo <= hi:
            p = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
            j = p - s + base
            v[p] += scale * (1.0 - h[j])
        lo = s
        if lo < 0: lo = 0
        hi = s + base
        if hi >= n: hi = n-1
        if lo <= hi:
            p = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
            j = p - s + base
            v[p] -= scale * h[j]
    return v
Could use `np.add.at` not needed. Because slices p are contiguous, j contiguous. We can use basic slicing: For first, if lo<=hi, j0 = lo - s + base, length=hi-lo+1. v[lo:hi+1] += scale*(1-h[j0:j0+len]). Similarly second. This avoids arange allocation. Great.
def weighted_topq_gradient(h, lags):
    n=len(h); base=n-1; scale=1/len(lags); v=np.zeros(n)
    for s in lags:
        f_lo = max(0, s-base); f_hi = min(n-1,s)
        if f_lo <= f_hi:
            j0 = f_lo - s + base
            v[f_lo:f_hi+1] += scale*(1.0 - h[j0:j0+(f_hi-f_lo+1)])
        b_lo = max(0, s); b_hi = min(n-1, s+base)
        if b_lo <= b_hi:
            j0 = b_lo - s + base
            v[b_lo:b_hi+1] -= scale*h[j0:j0+(b_hi-b_lo+1)]
    return v

Let's refine `topq_gradient_flow`.
Pseudo:
def topq_flow(h, n, q_list, deadline):
    target = n/2.0
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')
    c5 = c.max()*2/n
    best_h, best_c5 = h.copy(), c5
    for q in q_list:
       # maybe several sweeps
       no_improve=0
       max_iters = 80 if q<=10 else 40
       for it in range(max_iters):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          if q >= len(c): idx = np.arange(len(c))
          else:
             # use top q based on c; maybe include tied near max
             idx = np.argpartition(c, -q)[-q:]
          v = weighted_topq_gradient(h, idx)  # average
          v -= v.mean()
          nv = np.linalg.norm(v)
          if nv < 1e-10: break
          # Try step sizes; step = amp/norm * v? L2 step amp.
          amps = [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005] times maybe
          accepted=False
          for amp in amps:
             cand = h - (amp/nv)*v
             cand = project_box_sum(cand, target)
             cc = np.correlate(cand, 1-cand, 'full')
             val = cc.max()*2/n
             if val < c5 - 1e-14:
                 h, c, c5, cc = cand, cc, val, cc
                 best...
                 accepted=True; break
          if not accepted:
             # maybe shrink amplist or break
             no_improve+=1
             if no_improve>2: break
       if c5<best_c5: update
       if c5 <= target_bound? maybe stop
    return best.

Pseudo:
def momentum_flow(h,n,beta,q,steps,deadline):
   v_mom = np.zeros(n)
   for it in range(steps):
      if timeout break
      compute idx and weights
      g = weighted_gradient / scale
      g -= mean(g)
      v_mom = beta_m*v_mom + (1-beta_m)*g
      # normalize? step vector = v_mom
      nv=norm(v_mom); if small break
      for amp in amps:
         cand=project(h - amp/nv*v_mom)
         val=...; if val<c5-tol accept and break
      else break
   return

def fix_sum(h, target):
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # add to interior coordinates
       int_mask = (h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if int_mask.any(): h[int_mask] += diff/int_mask.sum()
       else:
          # distribute to not at bound or scale
          # diff small, maybe adjust all by diff/n but clip then repeat? Use project_box_sum(h+diff/n)
          h = project_box_sum(h+diff/n,target)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h

Pseudo:
def solve_coord(c,h,p,n):
  base=n-1; x0=h[p]
  lower=min(p, base-p); upper=max(p+base, 2*base-p)
  ms_len=upper-lower+1
  a=np.zeros(ms_len)
  s1=max(lower, base-p); s2=min(upper, 2*base-p)

Let's detail `project_box_sum`:
def project_box_sum(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    n = len(z)
    # Quick if already feasible
    if z.min() >= -1e-15 and z.max() <= 1+1e-15:
        diff = target - z.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            return np.clip(z,0,1)
    # bisection for lambda: y = clip(z - lambda)
    # Need low lambda -> high sum, high lambda -> low sum.
    mn = float(z.min()); mx = float(z.max())
    span = max(1.0, mx - mn)
    lo = mn - span - 1.0  # lambda low, y all 1? Check: lambda low, z-lambda large? If lambda = mn - span -1, for largest z=mx: mx - (mn-span-1)=mx-mn+span+1 = 2span+1 >1 -> y=1. good.
    hi = mx + span + 1.0  # lambda high, mn - hi negative -> y=0. good.
    for _ in range(80):
       lam = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       y = np.clip(z - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
       s = y.sum()
       if s > target:
           lo = lam  # need larger lambda to reduce sum
       else:
           hi = lam
    lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
    y=np.clip(z-lam,0,1)
    # fix residual
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-10:
       mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any():
          y[mask] += diff/mask.sum()
          y=np.clip(y,0,1)
       # if still, add to all? Use another bisection? diff tiny.
    return y
Check: y=clip(z-lambda). As lambda increases, y decreases. If s>target, lambda too low, set lo=lam (higher). correct. At lo, s maybe n; hi 0.
Residual after 80 bisection ~ (range)/2^80 tiny. mask addition exact.

But if z has large negative/positive, span huge, bisection still okay. 80 maybe overkill but fast (80*700=56k). Projection called many times; 80 maybe heavy. Use 60 iterations enough (range/1e18). 50 enough. Use 60. We can optimize by if z already box feasible and sum diff small, adjust with waterfill? But okay.

LP phase:
def lp_phase(h,n,c,c5,qs,deadline,best):
  for q in qs:
    for it in range(30):
      if time>deadline break
      lags=top_indices(c,q)
      if len(lags)==0: break
      G=np.empty((len(lags),n))
      for i,s in enumerate(lags): G[i]=lag_gradient(h,s)
      row_norm=np.linalg.norm(G, axis=1, ord=np.inf)
      row_norm[row_norm==0]=1
      Gs=G/row_norm[:,None]
      c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[0]=-1
      A_ub = np.column_stack((-np.ones(len(lags)), -Gs)) # shape q,n+1
      b_ub=np.zeros(len(lags))
      A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,1:]=1.0
      b_eq=np.zeros(1)
      bounds=[(0.0, 100.0)] + [(-max(h[i],0)+? Wait bound lower -h_i? We want δ can move h: candidate h_i + factor*δ. LP δ itself can be within [-h_i, 1-h_i] to keep step1 feasible. Use lower=-h[i]-1e-12, upper=1-h[i]+1e-12. If h near 0, lower ~ -1e-12; can't decrease. Good.
      res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub,b_ub,A_eq,b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
      if not res.success: continue
      tau=res.x[0]; delta=res.x[1:]
      if tau <= 1e-6: continue
      # line search factors
      accepted=False
      for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
         cand = h + fac*delta
         cand = project_box_sum(cand,n/2.0)
         cc=corr_counts(cand); val=c5_from_counts(cc,n)
         if val < best_c - 1e-13:
             h,c,c5= cand,cc,val; best update; accepted=True; break
      if not accepted: break
    if improved break? continue all q.

Topq_flow:
def topq_flow(h,n,q_list,deadline,best_c):
  target=n/2
  c=corr_counts(h); c5=max...
  for q in q_list:
    steps=250 if q<=10 else 80? Need time.
    no_improve=0
    v_mom=np.zeros(n)
    amp=0.05
    for it in range(steps):
      if time>deadline break
      lags=top_indices(c,q)
      if len(lags)==0: break
      g=weighted_topq_gradient(h,lags)
      g -= g.mean()
      nv=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if nv<1e-12: break
      # scale g to unit? step_size = amp
      # maybe momentum:
      # v_mom = 0.9*v_mom + 0.1*(g/nv?)
      # But g scale maybe top count gradient ~O(1). Use normalized direction dir=-g/nv.
      dir = -g/nv
      # Try several amplitudes decreasing:
      accepted=False
      for step in (amp, amp*0.5, amp*0.25, amp*0.1, amp*0.05, amp*0.01):
          cand = project_box_sum(h + step*dir, target)
          cc=corr_counts(cand); val=...
          if val < c5 - 1e-13:
             h,c,c5=cand,cc,val; best...; accepted=True; break
      if accepted:
          amp = min(0.2, amp*1.1)
          no_improve=0
      else:
          amp *=0.5
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve>4 or amp<1e-5: break
    if c5<best... update
  return h,c,c5

CD polish function:
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline,rng):
  target=n/2
  c=corr_counts(h); c5=...
  for pass in range(passes):
    if time>deadline: break
    order = rng.permutation(n) if random else np.arange(n)
    # maybe sample 300 random coordinates per pass to be faster
    for p in order[:n]:
       if time>deadline: break
       update_coord(c,h,p,n)
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=corr_counts(h); c5=...
  return h,c,c5

def cd_pass_once(h,n,order,deadline):
   h0=h.copy(); c=corr_counts(h0); c0=c.max()*dx
   # mutate copy
   h_w=h0.copy(); c_w=c.copy()
   for p in order:
      if time>deadline: break
      update_coord(c_w,h_w,p,n)

   c_new=corr_counts(h_w); c5_new=...

   else return h0,c,c0,False

Let's write `update_coord` carefully with vector operations to minimize overhead.

Pseudo code:
def update_coord(c,h,p,n):
    base = n-1
    x0 = h[p]
    lower = p if p < base-p else base-p  # min
    # lower = min(p, base-p)
    if p <= base-p: lower=p
    else: lower=base-p
    # upper = max(p+base, 2*base-p)
    u1 = p + base
    u2 = 2*base - p
    upper = u1 if u1 > u2 else u2
    L = lower
    U = upper
    size = U-L+1
    a = np.zeros(size, dtype=float)
    # first
    s1 = base - p
    if s1 < L: s1 = L
    s2 = 2*base - p
    if s2 > U: s2 = U
    if s1 <= s2:
       idx1=s1-L; ln=s2-s1+1
       j0=p+s1-base
       a[idx1:idx1+ln] += 1.0 - h[j0:j0+ln]
    # second
    s1 = p
    if s1 < L: s1 = L
    s2 = p + base
    if s2 > U: s2 = U
    if s1 <= s2:
       idx1=s1-L; ln=s2-s1+1
       j0=p - s1 + base
       a[idx1:idx1+ln] -= h[j0:j0+ln]
    c_base = c[L:U+1]
    has_diag = (L <= base <= U)
    diag_rel = base-L if has_diag else -1
    # unaff max
    unaff = 0.0
    if L > 0:
       m = float(np.max(c[:L])); if m>unaff: unaff=m
    if U < 2*base:
       m = float(np.max(c[U+1:])); if m>unaff: unaff=m
    # masks, exclude diag from zero/pos/neg? pos/neg include diag? Diagonal has nonlinear, but linear part still exists. For bisection feasibility, treat diagonal separately; but its linear part also contributes? We used q(y) = c_d + a_d y - y^2 ≤L. We should not also apply linear constraints for diag with a_d separately, because q includes a_d y. If we include diag in pos/neg, we impose c_d + a_d y ≤L, stronger than actual? For non-diag linear; for diag, the nonlinear term -y^2 makes actual ≤ c_d+a_dy, so if actual≤L then linear also≤L? Since -y^2 ≤0, actual < linear. The linear constraint is stricter; could exclude feasible y where linear>L but quadratic brings below? Is that possible? q = linear - y^2. If linear > L, q could be ≤L if y^2 > linear-L. For large y, yes. Excluding diagonal from pos/neg is correct. We'll exclude from pos/neg/zero.
    pos = a > 1e-14
    neg = a < -1e-14
    zero = np.abs(a) <= 1e-14
    if has_diag:
       pos[diag_rel]=False; neg[diag_rel]=False; zero[diag_rel]=False
    # current local M0
    M0 = unaff
    if c_base.size:
       mb=float(np.max(c_base)); if mb>M0: M0=mb
    L_hi=M0
    L_lo=unaff
    # If M0 close unaff? still.
    # Bisection
    # To reduce iterations, if M0 <= unaff + 1e-14: no affected reduces max? return False
    if L_hi <= L_lo + 1e-14:
       return False
    for _ in range(35):
       if L_hi - L_lo <= 1e-12: break
       Lmid=0.5*(L_hi+L_lo)
       feas=True
       x_lo=0.0; x_hi=1.0
       if zero_any? To avoid calling np.any repeatedly? We can precompute masks and maybe if no. But fine.
       if np.any(zero):
          if np.any(c_base[zero] > Lmid + 1e-12): feas=False
       if feas and np.any(pos):
          # x ≤ x0 + (L-c)/a
          bounds = x0 + (Lmid - c_base[pos]) / a[pos]
          x_hi = min(x_hi, float(np.min(bounds)))
       if feas and np.any(neg):
          bounds = x0 + (Lmid - c_base[neg]) / a[neg]
          x_lo = max(x_lo, float(np.max(bounds)))
       if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-12: feas=False
       if feas and has_diag:
          a_d=float(a[diag_rel]); c_d=float(c_base[diag_rel])
          disc=a_d*a_d + 4.0*(c_d - Lmid)
          if disc >= 0.0:
             sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(a_d-sq)*0.5; r2=(a_d+sq)*0.5
             f_lo=x0+r1; f_hi=x0+r2
             if f_hi > 0.0 and f_lo < 1.0:
                # if the current feasible x interval is entirely inside forbidden (strict)
                if x_lo > f_lo and x_hi < f_hi:
                   feas=False
                # If interval overlaps but covers outside, feasible.
       if feas: L_hi=Lmid
       else: L_lo=Lmid
    L=L_hi
    # reconstruct interval at L
    x_lo=0.0; x_hi=1.0
    if np.any(pos):
       x_hi = min(x_hi, float(np.min(x0 + (L - c_base[pos]) / a[pos])))
    if np.any(neg):
       x_lo = max(x_lo, float(np.max(x0 + (L - c_base[neg]) / a[neg])))
    x_lo=max(x_lo,0.0); x_hi=min(x_hi,1.0)
    if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-12:
       x_lo,x_hi=x0,x0
    candidates=[]
    if has_diag:
       a_d=float(a[diag_rel]); c_d=float(c_base[diag_rel])
       disc=a_d*a_d + 4.0*(c_d - L)
       f_lo=x0; f_hi=x0
       if disc >= 0.0:
          sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(a_d-sq)*0.5; r2=(a_d+sq)*0.5
          f_lo=x0+r1; f_hi=x0+r2
       if f_hi <= 0.0 or f_lo >= 1.0 or disc < 0.0:
          mid=0.5*(x_lo+x_hi)
          candidates.append(mid)
       else:
          # left [x_lo, min(x_hi,f_lo)]
          lo1=x_lo; hi1=min(x_hi,f_lo)
          if hi1 >= lo1 - 1e-12: candidates.append(0.5*(lo1+hi1))
          lo2=max(x_lo,f_hi); hi2=x_hi
          if hi2 >= lo2 - 1e-12: candidates.append(0.5*(lo2+hi2))
    else:
       candidates.append(0.5*(x_lo+x_hi))
    candidates.append(x0)
    candidates.append(0.0); candidates.append(1.0)
    best_x=x0; best_M=M0
    for x in candidates:
       if x<0.0 or x>1.0: continue
       d=x-x0
       vals = c_base + a*d
       if has_diag:
          vals[diag_rel] -= d*d
       m=float(np.max(vals))
       if m < M0: M0? Wait unaff.
       M=unaff if unaff>m else m
       if M < best_M - 1e-13:
          best_M=M; best_x=x
    if best_x == x0:
       # Maybe if x0 not feasible at L due diagonal? But x0 M0 feasible. If best_M==M0 and midpoint maybe same local, no update.
       return False
    # However best_x could be outside original x_lo? candidates mid within. okay.
    delta=best_x-x0
    h[p]=best_x
    c[L:U+1] += delta * a
    if has_diag:
       c[base] -= delta*delta
    return True
Need after `vals = c_base + a*d`, if has_diag, vals is new array? `c_base + a*d` creates new, so modifying vals[diag_rel] doesn't affect c_base. Good.

`topq_flow` details:
def topq_flow(h,n,qs,deadline, max_iter):
  target=0.5*n
  c=corr_counts(h); c5=...
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
  for q in qs:
    amp=0.05
    no=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       lags=top_indices(c,q)
       if len(lags)==0: break
       g=weighted_topq_gradient(h,lags)
       g-=g.mean()
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if scale<1e-14: break
       dirv = -g/scale
       accepted=False
       for step in (amp, 0.5*amp,0.25*amp,0.1*amp,0.05*amp,0.01*amp):
           cand=project_box_sum(h + step*dirv, target)
           cc=corr_counts(cand); val=...
           if val < c5 - 1e-13:
              h=cand; c=cc; c5=val
              if val < best_c - 1e-15: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=val
              accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           amp = min(0.2, amp*1.2)
           no=0
       else:
           amp *=0.5
           no+=1
           if no>5 or amp < 1e-5: break
  # ensure best_h feasible
  best_h=project_box_sum(best_h,target)
  return best_h, best_c

def top_indices(c,q):
    m=c.size
    if q<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    if q>=m: return np.arange(m)
    idx=np.argpartition(c, m-q)[m-q:]
    # include ties if not too many
    thresh=c.min() if False else ...
```

`weighted_topq_gradient`:
```
def weighted_topq_gradient(h,lags):
    n=h.size; base=n-1; v=np.zeros(n)
    if lags.size==0: return v
    sc=1.0/lags.size
    for s in lags:
        s=int(s)
        lo=max(0, base-s); hi=min(base, 2*base-s)
        if lo<=hi:
            j0=lo+s-base; ln=hi-lo+1
            v[lo:hi+1] += sc*(1.0-h[j0:j0+ln])
        lo=max(0, s-base); hi=min(base, s)
        if lo<=hi:
            j0=lo-s+base; ln=hi-lo+1
            v[lo:hi+1] -= sc*h[j0:j0+ln]
    return v
```
Check j0 for second when s could be >base: lo=s-base, j0=s-base-s+base=0; hi=min(base,s)=base, ln=base-lo+1=2base-s+1? j range 0..hi-s+base=base-s+base=2base-s ≤base. good.

`lag_gradient` similar without scale:
```
def lag_gradient(h,s):
  n=h.size; base=n-1; g=np.zeros(n); s=int(s)
  lo=max(0,base-s); hi=min(base,2*base-s)
  if lo<=hi: j0=lo+s-base; ln=...; g[lo:hi+1]+=1-h[j0:j0+ln]
  lo=max(0,s-base); hi=min(base,s)
  if lo<=hi: j0=lo-s+base; ln=...; g[lo:hi+1]-=h[j0:j0+ln]
  return g
```

`cd_pass_once`:
```
def cd_pass_once(h,n,order,deadline):
    target=0.5*n
    dx=2.0/n
    c=corr_counts(h)
    c0=float(c.max())*dx
    h_w=h.copy(); c_w=c.copy()
    for p in order:
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        if update_coord(c_w,h_w,p,n):
            pass
    h_w=project_box_sum(h_w,target)
    cn=corr_counts(h_w)
    c5n=float(cn.max())*dx
    if c5n < c0 - 1e-13:
        return h_w, c5n, True
    return h, c0, False
```
But `c` and `c0` computed before pass. If input h/c not passed. Fine.
If order full but time mid-pass, it will still project partial and maybe improve. Good.
Random order: if `order is None`, use np.arange(n). Else pass permutation.
We can call with subset random length min(n, 300) for quick. But full for final.

`cd_polish`:
```
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline,rng):
    best_h=h; best_c=get_c5...
    for i in range(passes):
       if time>deadline: break
       if i==0: order=np.arange(n)
       else: order=rng.permutation(n)
       h2,c2,improved=cd_pass_once(best_h,n,order,deadline)
       if improved:
          best_h,best_c=h2,c2
       else: break
       # do another sequential? continue
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need if no improvement stop to save.

`topq_flow`: It may call `project_box_sum` many times. We need pass deadline and max_iter per q.
```
def topq_flow(h,n,qs,deadline,max_iter=30):
    target=0.5*n; dx=2/n
    c=corr_counts(h); c5=float(c.max())*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
    for q in qs:
       amp=0.04
       no=0
       for it in range(max_iter):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          lags=top_indices(c,q)
          if lags.size==0: break
          g=weighted_topq_gradient(h,lags)
          g-=g.mean()
          sc=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
          if sc<1e-14: break
          dirv=-g/sc
          accepted=False
          for st in (amp,0.5*amp,0.25*amp,0.1*amp,0.05*amp,0.01*amp):
              cand=project_box_sum(h+st*dirv,target)
              cc=corr_counts(cand); val=float(cc.max())*dx
              if val + 1e-13 < c5:
                  h,c,c5=cand,cc,val
                  if val + 1e-15 < best_c: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=val
                  accepted=True; break
          if accepted:
             amp = min(0.25, amp*1.25); no=0
          else:
             amp *=0.5; no+=1
             if no>5 or amp<1e-5: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
Potential: local c5 can improve but not best; it continues from local even if not best. Good. If local worsens? We only accept improvements, so local never worsens. Good.

`lp_phase`:
```
def lp_phase(h,n,qs,deadline,max_iter=12):
    target=0.5*n; dx=2/n
    c=corr_counts(h); c5=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
    for q in qs:
       no=0
       for it in range(max_iter):
          if time>deadline: break
          lags=top_indices(c,q)
          if lags.size==0: break
          G=np.empty((lags.size,n))
          for i,s in enumerate(lags): G[i]=lag_gradient(h,s)
          rn=np.linalg.norm(G, axis=1, ord=np.inf)
          rn[rn<=1e-14]=1.0
          Gs=G/rn[:,None]
          m=lags.size
          c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[0]=-1.0
          A_ub=np.column_stack((np.ones(m), Gs))
          b_ub=np.zeros(m)
          A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,1:]=1.0
          b_eq=np.zeros(1)
          bounds=[(0.0, 100.0)]
          # bounds for delta:
          lo = (-h) - 1e-9; lo[lo<-1]=-1; hi=(1.0-h)+1e-9; hi[hi>1]=1
          # If h at bound, allow tiny outward? For factor<1 line search, bounds too tight? use exact room plus small.
          for i in range(n): bounds.append((lo[i], hi[i]))
          try:
             res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub,b_ub,A_eq,b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
          except Exception:
             break
          if not res.success: break
          if res.x is None or res.x[0] <= 1e-7: break
          delta=res.x[1:]
          # if delta all zero break
          accepted=False
          for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
              cand=project_box_sum(h+fac*delta,target)
              cc=corr_counts(cand); val=float(cc.max())*dx
              if val +1e-13 < c5:
                 h,c,c5=cand,cc,val; best...; accepted=True; break
          if accepted: no=0
          else:
             no+=1
             if no>3: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
Building bounds list with loop 700 each LP; okay. Could vectorize but fine.
`linprog` may return success but tau small; break to avoid same.
If q changes, maybe direction. Good.

LP constraints with row scaled by inf norm: If row norm =2, Gs entries scaled to [-1,1]. δ bounded by room. Max τ maybe ~? Good.

One subtlety: For linear approximation of c_s, if diagonal s=base, gradient row g is derivative (1-2h). But actual change includes -delta^2. LP linear may overestimate reduction for diagonal (since -delta^2 is extra reduction when delta not zero? Actually c_diag new = c + gδ - δ^2, linear term gδ, actual less than linear if δ^2 positive. If linear says decrease, actual decreases more, good. If linear says increase, actual might still decrease for large δ due -δ^2? But δ small; fine.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(hsrc,nsrc,nt):
    xs=np.linspace(0,2,nsrc,endpoint=False); xt=np.linspace(0,2,nt,endpoint=False)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(xt,xs,hsrc),0.5*nt)
```

def make_initial(n):
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    h=0.5 + 0.3*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.1*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    return project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)

Pseudo run:
```
def run(...):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3
    ...
    # 1 LP
    if time.time() < deadline - 200:
        h,c=lp_phase(h_best,n,[3,5,10,20],min(deadline,time.time()+150),8)
        if c<best_c: ...
    # 2 TQ
    if time.time() < deadline - 180:
        h,c=topq_flow(h_best,n,[5,10,20],min(deadline,time.time()+120),20)
        if c<best_c: ...
    # 3 CD
    h,c=cd_polish(h_best,n,3,deadline-30,rng)
    if c<best_c: ...
    # 4 alternating
    guard=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 90 and guard<8:
       guard+=1
       h,c=lp_phase(h_best,n,[5,10,20],min(deadline-60,time.time()+45),3)
       if c<best_c: ...
       h,c=cd_polish(h_best,n,1,min(deadline-30,time.time()+45),rng)
       if c<best_c: ...
       else:
          if time.time() > deadline-120: break
    # 5 random perturb
    for scale in (0.003,0.008,0.015):
       if time.time() > deadline - 80: break
       hp=project_box_sum(h_best + rng.normal(0.0,scale,n),0.5*n)
       hp,pc=lp_phase(hp,n,[5,10],min(deadline-50,time.time()+40),3)
       hp,pc=topq_flow(hp,n,[5,10],min(deadline-30,time.time()+30),10)
       hp,pc=cd_polish(hp,n,1,min(deadline-10,time.time()+20),rng)
       if pc<best_c: ...
    # 6 different n
    for ntry in ...:
       if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
       hp=resample_h(...); hp,pc=cd_polish(hp,ntry,1,deadline-5,rng)
       if pc<best_c: ...
    # final polish
    if time.time()<deadline-3:
       hp,pc=cd_polish(h_best,n,1,deadline-1,rng)
       if pc<best_c: ...
    h_best=project_box_sum(h_best,0.5*n)
    c=corr_counts(h_best); best_c=float(c.max())*2/n
    return h_best,best_c,n
```

Pseudo final code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def project_box_sum(z, target):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    n=z.size
    if n==0: return z
    mn=float(z.min()); mx=float(z.max()); span=max(1.0,mx-mn)
    lo=mn-span-1.0; hi=mx+span+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        lam=(lo+hi)*0.5
        s=np.clip(z-lam,0.0,1.0).sum()
        if s>target: lo=lam
        else: hi=lam
    y=np.clip(z-(lo+hi)*0.5,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-10:
        mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1.0-1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
            y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    return y
```
Could first quick if feasible to save? Not needed but can.

`make_initial`:
```
def make_initial(n):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    h=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.1*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x) - 0.05*np.cos(3.0*np.pi*x)
    return project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
```

`update_coord` detailed. Need ensure if `size=0`? never.
Use `tol=1e-12`. In bisection, `c_base[zero] > Lmid + 1e-11` maybe.
Candidate list: To avoid modifying while iterating, just append.
```
def update_coord(c,h,p,n):
    base=n-1
    x0=float(h[p])
    low1=p; low2=base-p
    lower=low1 if low1<low2 else low2
    up1=p+base; up2=2*base-p
    upper=up1 if up1>up2 else up2
    size=upper-lower+1
    a=np.zeros(size)
    s1=p; s2=p+base
    if s1<lower: s1=lower
    if s2>upper: s2=upper
    if s1<=s2:
       idx=s1-lower; ln=s2-s1+1
       j_hi=p+base-s1; j_lo=p+base-s2
       a[idx:idx+ln] += 1.0 - h[j_lo:j_hi+1][::-1]
    s1=base-p; s2=2*base-p
    if s1<lower: s1=lower
    if s2>upper: s2=upper
    if s1<=s2:
       idx=s1-lower; ln=s2-s1+1
       j0=p+s1-base
       a[idx:idx+ln] -= h[j0:j0+ln]
    cbase=c[lower:upper+1]
    diag = 1 if lower<=base<=upper else 0
    irel=base-lower if diag else -1
    unaff=0.0
    if lower>0:
       m=float(np.max(c[:lower])); unaff=m
    if upper < 2*base:
       m=float(np.max(c[upper+1:]));
       if m>unaff: unaff=m
    cmax=float(np.max(cbase))
    M0=unaff if unaff>cmax else cmax
    if M0 <= unaff + 1e-13:
       return False
    pos=a>1e-14; neg=a<-1e-14; zero=np.abs(a)<=1e-14
    if diag:
       pos[irel]=False; neg[irel]=False; zero[irel]=False
    hp=bool(np.any(pos)); hn=bool(np.any(neg)); hz=bool(np.any(zero))
    Llo=unaff; Lhi=M0
    for _ in range(35):
       if Lhi-Llo < 1e-12: break
       Lm=(Lhi+Llo)*0.5
       feas=True
       xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
       if hz and np.any(cbase[zero] > Lm + 1e-11): feas=False
       if feas and hp:
           xb=float(np.min(x0 + (Lm-cbase[pos])/a[pos]))
           if xb<xhi: xhi=xb
       if feas and hn:
           xb=float(np.max(x0 + (Lm-cbase[neg])/a[neg]))
           if xb>xlo: xlo=xb
       if xlo > xhi + 1e-11: feas=False
       if feas and diag:
           ad=float(a[irel]); cd=float(cbase[irel])
           disc=ad*ad + 4.0*(cd-Lm)
           if disc >= 0.0:
              sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(ad-sq)*0.5; r2=(ad+sq)*0.5
              flo=x0+r1; fhi=x0+r2
              if fhi > 0.0 and flo < 1.0 and xlo > flo and xhi < fhi:
                  feas=False
       if feas: Lhi=Lm
       else: Llo=Lm
    L=Lhi
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
    if hp: xhi=min(xhi,float(np.min(x0+(L-cbase[pos])/a[pos])))
    if hn: xlo=max(xlo,float(np.max(x0+(L-cbase[neg])/a[neg])))
    if xlo<0: xlo=0
    if xhi>1: xhi=1
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-11: xlo=xhi=x0
    cands=[x0,0.0,1.0]
    if diag:
       ad=float(a[irel]); cd=float(cbase[irel])
       disc=ad*ad + 4.0*(cd-L)
       if disc >=0:
          sq=np.sqrt(disc); flo=x0+(ad-sq)*0.5; fhi=x0+(ad+sq)*0.5
          if fhi>0 and flo<1:
             # left
             l1=xlo; h1=flo if flo<xhi else xhi
             if h1>=l1-1e-12: cands.append(0.5*(l1+h1)); cands.append(l1); cands.append(h1)
             l2=fhi if fhi>xlo else xlo; h2=xhi
             if h2>=l2-1e-12: cands.append(0.5*(l2+h2)); cands.append(l2); cands.append(h2)
             cands.append(0.5*(xlo+xhi))
          else:
             cands.append(0.5*(xlo+xhi))
    else:
       cands.append(0.5*(xlo+xhi)); cands.append(xlo); cands.append(xhi)
    best_x=x0; best_M=M0
    for x in cands:
       if x<0 or x>1: continue
       d=x-x0
       vals=cbase + a*d
       if diag: vals[irel] -= d*d
       m=float(np.max(vals))
       if m<unaff: m=unaff
       if m + 1e-13 < best_M:
          best_M=m; best_x=x
    if best_x == x0: return False
    delta=best_x-x0
    h[p]=best_x
    c[lower:upper+1] += delta*a
    if diag: c[base] -= delta*delta
    return True
```
Potential issue: In positive first range, using `h[j_lo:j_hi+1][::-1]` when j_lo==j_hi: slice returns array length1, reverse ok. If j_lo/j_hi int. Good.

`cd_pass_once` and `cd_polish` need pass `deadline`. In `cd_pass_once`, if order is generator? We'll pass np array.
```
def cd_pass_once(h,n,order,deadline):
    target=0.5*n; dx=2.0/n
    c=corr_counts(h); c0=float(c.max())*dx
    h_w=h.copy(); c_w=c.copy()
    for p in order:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       update_coord(c_w,h_w,int(p),n)
    h_w=project_box_sum(h_w,target)
    cn=corr_counts(h_w); c5n=float(cn.max())*dx
    if c5n + 1e-13 < c0:
       return h_w,c5n,True
    return h,c0(False)
```
But if order is None, use arange. In cd_polish:
```
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline,rng):
    best_h=h; c=corr_counts(best_h); best_c=float(c.max())*(2/n)
    for i in range(passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if i==0: order=np.arange(n)
       else: order=rng.permutation(n)
       h2,c2,ok=cd_pass_once(best_h,n,order,deadline)
       if ok:
          best_h,best_c=h2,c2
       else:
          break
    return best_h,best_c
```
If pass doesn't improve but another random order might, break stops. Maybe don't break; try next random? If one pass no improve, subsequent random unlikely if exact CD no strict. But topq/LP may. We can break to save.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-3.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h_best=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       h_best=make_initial(700)
    n=len(h_best)
    h_best=project_box_sum(h_best,0.5*n)
    c=corr_counts(h_best); best_c=float(c.max())*(2.0/n)
    # 1 LP main
    if time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
       h2,c2=lp_phase(h_best,n,(3,5,10,20,40),min(deadline,time.time()+140.0),6)
       if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
    # 2 topq
    if time.time() < deadline - 200.0:
       h2,c2=topq_flow(h_best,n,(5,10,20,40),min(deadline,time.time()+120.0),18)
       if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
    # 3 cd
    if time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
       h2,c2=cd_polish(h_best,n,2,deadline-40.0,rng)
       if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
    # alternate
    for _ in range(6):
       if time.time() > deadline - 100.0: break
       h2,c2=lp_phase(h_best,n,(5,10,20),min(deadline-80.0,time.time()+35.0),2)
       if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
       h2,c2=cd_polish(h_best,n,1,min(deadline-40.0,time.time()+35.0),rng)
       if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
    # random perturb
    scales=(0.002,0.005,0.01)
    for i,scale in enumerate(scales):
       if time.time() > deadline - 120.0: break
       target=0.5*n
       hp=project_box_sum(h_best + rng.normal(0.0,scale,n),target)
       hp,pc=lp_phase(hp,n,(5,10),min(deadline-70.0,time.time()+35.0),2)
       hp,pc=topq_flow(hp,n,(5,10),min(deadline-50.0,time.time()+25.0),6)
       hp,pc=cd_polish(hp,n,1,min(deadline-15.0,time.time()+15.0),rng)
       if pc + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=hp,pc
    # different n
    ntries=(n+20,n-20,760,680,620)
    for nt in ntries:
       if time.time() > deadline - 70.0: break
       if nt<200 or nt>1000 or nt==n: continue
       hp=resample_h(h_best,n,nt)
       hp,pc=cd_polish(hp,nt,1,deadline-20.0,rng)
       if pc + 1e-14 < best_c:
           h_best,best_c,n=hp,pc,nt
    # final cd
    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
       h2,c2=cd_polish(h_best,n,1,deadline-1.0,rng)
       if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c: h_best,best_c=h2,c2
    h_best=project_box_sum(h_best,0.5*n)
    c=corr_counts(h_best); best_c=float(c.max())*(2.0/n)
    return h_best, float(best_c), int(n)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def project_box_sum(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    if z.min() >= -1e-12 and z.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        zc = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(zc.sum() - target) < 1e-9:
            return zc
    mn = float(z.min())
    mx = float(z.max())
    span = max(1.0, mx - mn)
    lo = mn - span - 1.0
    hi = mx + span + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(z - lam, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = lam
        else:
            hi = lam
    y = np.clip(z - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
        mask = (y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            y[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y


def corr_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5_from_counts(c, n):
    return float(c.max() * (2.0 / n))


def get_c5(h, n):
    c = corr_counts(h)
    return c, c5_from_counts(c, n)


def make_initial(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    h = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.10 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.05 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)


def top_indices(c, q):
    m = c.size
    if q <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if q >= m:
        return np.arange(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - q)[m - q:]
    thresh = c[idx].min()
    extra = np.where(c >= thresh - 1e-12)[0]
    if extra.size <= 3 * q + 20:
        idx = extra
    return idx


def weighted_topq_gradient(h, lags):
    n = h.size
    base = n - 1
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if lags.size == 0:
        return v
    scale = 1.0 / float(lags.size)
    for s in lags:
        s = int(s)
        lo = max(0, s - base)
        hi = min(base, s)
        if lo <= hi:
            j0 = lo + base - s
            ln = hi - lo + 1
            v[lo:hi + 1] += scale * (1.0 - h[j0:j0 + ln])
        lo = max(0, base - s)
        hi = min(base, 2 * base - s)
        if lo <= hi:
            j0 = lo + s - base
            ln = hi - lo + 1
            v[lo:hi + 1] -= scale * h[j0:j0 + ln]
    return v


def lag_gradient(h, s):
    n = h.size
    base = n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    s = int(s)
    lo = max(0, s - base)
    hi = min(base, s)
    if lo <= hi:
        j0 = lo + base - s
        ln = hi - lo + 1
        g[lo:hi + 1] += 1.0 - h[j0:j0 + ln]
    lo = max(0, base - s)
    hi = min(base, 2 * base - s)
    if lo <= hi:
        j0 = lo + s - base
        ln = hi - lo + 1
        g[lo:hi + 1] -= h[j0:j0 + ln]
    return g


def update_coord(c, h, p, n):
    base = n - 1
    x0 = float(h[p])
    a1, a2 = base - p, p
    lower = a1 if a1 < a2 else a2
    b1, b2 = p + base, 2 * base - p
    upper = b1 if b1 > b2 else b2
    size = upper - lower + 1
    a = np.zeros(size, dtype=float)

    s1 = p
    s2 = p + base
    if s1 < lower:
        s1 = lower
    if s2 > upper:
        s2 = upper
    if s1 <= s2:
        idx = s1 - lower
        ln = s2 - s1 + 1
        j_hi = p + base - s1
        j_lo = p + base - s2
        a[idx:idx + ln] += 1.0 - h[j_lo:j_hi + 1][::-1]

    s1 = base - p
    s2 = 2 * base - p
    if s1 < lower:
        s1 = lower
    if s2 > upper:
        s2 = upper
    if s1 <= s2:
        idx = s1 - lower
        ln = s2 - s1 + 1
        j0 = p + s1 - base
        a[idx:idx + ln] -= h[j0:j0 + ln]

    cbase = c[lower:upper + 1]
    has_diag = (lower <= base <= upper)
    irel = base - lower if has_diag else -1

    unaff = 0.0
    if lower > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:lower]))
    if upper < 2 * base:
        m = float(np.max(c[upper + 1:]))
        if m > unaff:
            unaff = m

    cur_affected = float(np.max(cbase))
    M0 = unaff if unaff > cur_affected else cur_affected
    if M0 <= unaff + 1e-13:
        return False

    pos = a > 1e-14
    neg = a < -1e-14
    zero = np.abs(a) <= 1e-14
    if has_diag:
        pos[irel] = False
        neg[irel] = False
        zero[irel] = False
    has_pos = bool(np.any(pos))
    has_neg = bool(np.any(neg))
    has_zero = bool(np.any(zero))

    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = M0
    for _ in range(35):
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-12:
            break
        Lm = 0.5 * (Lhi + Llo)
        feas = True
        xlo = 0.0
        xhi = 1.0
        if has_zero and np.any(cbase[zero] > Lm + 1e-11):
            feas = False
        if feas and has_pos:
            xb = float(np.min(x0 + (Lm - cbase[pos]) / a[pos]))
            if xb < xhi:
                xhi = xb
        if feas and has_neg:
            xb = float(np.max(x0 + (Lm - cbase[neg]) / a[neg]))
            if xb > xlo:
                xlo = xb
        if xlo > xhi + 1e-11:
            feas = False
        if feas and has_diag:
            ad = float(a[irel])
            cd = float(cbase[irel])
            disc = ad * ad + 4.0 * (cd - Lm)
            if disc >= 0.0:
                sq = np.sqrt(disc)
                flo = x0 + 0.5 * (ad - sq)
                fhi = x0 + 0.5 * (ad + sq)
                if fhi > 0.0 and flo < 1.0 and xlo > flo and xhi < fhi:
                    feas = False
        if feas:
            Lhi = Lm
        else:
            Llo = Lm

    L = Lhi
    xlo = 0.0
    xhi = 1.0
    if has_pos:
        xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min(x0 + (L - cbase[pos]) / a[pos])))
    if has_neg:
        xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max(x0 + (L - cbase[neg]) / a[neg])))
    xlo = max(0.0, xlo)
    xhi = min(1.0, xhi)
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-11:
        xlo = xhi = x0

    candidates = [x0, 0.0, 1.0]
    if has_diag:
        ad = float(a[irel])
        cd = float(cbase[irel])
        disc = ad * ad + 4.0 * (cd - L)
        if disc >= 0.0:
            sq = np.sqrt(disc)
            flo = x0 + 0.5 * (ad - sq)
            fhi = x0 + 0.5 * (ad + sq)
            if fhi > 0.0 and flo < 1.0:
                l1 = xlo
                h1 = flo if flo < xhi else xhi
                if h1 >= l1 - 1e-12:
                    candidates.append(0.5 * (l1 + h1))
                    candidates.append(l1)
                    candidates.append(h1)
                l2 = fhi if fhi > xlo else xlo
                h2 = xhi
                if h2 >= l2 - 1e-12:
                    candidates.append(0.5 * (l2 + h2))
                    candidates.append(l2)
                    candidates.append(h2)
            candidates.append(0.5 * (xlo + xhi))
    else:
        candidates.append(0.5 * (xlo + xhi))
        candidates.append(xlo)
        candidates.append(xhi)

    best_x = x0
    best_m = M0
    for x in candidates:
        if x < 0.0 or x > 1.0:
            continue
        d = x - x0
        vals = cbase + a * d
        if has_diag:
            vals[irel] -= d * d
        m = float(np.max(vals))
        if m < unaff:
            m = unaff
        if m + 1e-13 < best_m:
            best_m = m
            best_x = x

    if best_x == x0:
        return False
    delta = best_x - x0
    h[p] = best_x
    c[lower:upper + 1] += delta * a
    if has_diag:
        c[base] -= delta * delta
    return True


def cd_pass_once(h, n, order, deadline):
    target = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c0 = corr_counts(h)
    base = float(c0.max()) * dx
    hw = h.copy()
    cw = c0.copy()
    for p in order:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        update_coord(cw, hw, int(p), n)
    hw = project_box_sum(hw, target)
    cn = corr_counts(hw)
    val = float(cn.max()) * dx
    if val + 1e-13 < base:
        return hw, val, True
    return h, base, False


def cd_polish(h, n, passes, deadline, rng):
    c = corr_counts(h)
    best_h = h
    best_c = c5_from_counts(c, n)
    for i in range(passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        order = np.arange(n) if i == 0 else rng.permutation(n)
        h2, c2, ok = cd_pass_once(best_h, n, order, deadline)
        if not ok:
            break
        best_h, best_c = h2, c2
    return best_h, best_c


def topq_flow(h, n, qs, deadline, max_iter=18):
    target = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = corr_counts(h)
    c5 = c5_from_counts(c, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5
    for q in qs:
        amp = 0.04
        no_improve = 0
        for _ in range(max_iter):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            lags = top_indices(c, q)
            if lags.size == 0:
                break
            if lags.size > 80:
                lags = lags[:80]
            g = weighted_topq_gradient(h, lags)
            g -= g.mean()
            sc = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if sc < 1e-14:
                break
            direction = -g / sc
            accepted = False
            for step in (amp, 0.5 * amp, 0.25 * amp, 0.1 * amp, 0.05 * amp, 0.01 * amp):
                cand = project_box_sum(h + step * direction, target)
                cc = corr_counts(cand)
                val = c5_from_counts(cc, n)
                if val + 1e-13 < c5:
                    h, c, c5 = cand, cc, val
                    if val + 1e-15 < best_c:
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = val
                    accepted = True
                    break
            if accepted:
                amp = min(0.25, amp * 1.25)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                amp *= 0.5
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 5 or amp < 1e-5:
                    break
    project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * n)
    return best_h, best_c


def lp_phase(h, n, qs, deadline, max_iter=6):
    target = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = corr_counts(h)
    c5 = c5_from_counts(c, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5
    for q in qs:
        no_improve = 0
        for _ in range(max_iter):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            lags = top_indices(c, q)
            if lags.size == 0:
                break
            if lags.size > 40:
                lags = lags[:40]
            m = lags.size
            G = np.empty((m, n), dtype=float)
            rows = []
            for i in range(m):
                g = lag_gradient(h, lags[i])
                rows.append(g)
            G = np.vstack(rows) if m else np.empty((0, n))
            rn = np.linalg.norm(G, axis=1, ord=np.inf)
            rn[rn <= 1e-14] = 1.0
            Gs = G / rn[:, None]
            c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
            c_obj[0] = -1.0
            A_ub = np.column_stack((np.ones(m, dtype=float), Gs))
            b_ub = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
            A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
            A_eq[0, 1:] = 1.0
            b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)
            lo = -h - 1e-9
            hi = 1.0 - h + 1e-9
            lo = np.clip(lo, -1.0, 1.0)
            hi = np.clip(hi, -1.0, 1.0)
            bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)]
            for i in range(n):
                bounds.append((float(lo[i]), float(hi[i])))
            try:
                res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                              bounds=bounds, method="highs")
            except Exception:
                try:
                    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                                  bounds=bounds)
                except Exception:
                    break
            if not getattr(res, "success", False):
                break
            if res.x is None or res.x[0] <= 1e-7:
                break
            delta = res.x[1:]
            accepted = False
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                cand = project_box_sum(h + fac * delta, target)
                cc = corr_counts(cand)
                val = c5_from_counts(cc, n)
                if val + 1e-13 < c5:
                    h, c, c5 = cand, cc, val
                    if val + 1e-15 < best_c:
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = val
                    accepted = True
                    break
            if accepted:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 2:
                    break
    project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * n)
    return best_h, best_c


def resample_h(hsrc, nsrc, nt):
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nsrc, endpoint=False)
    xt = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nt, endpoint=False)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(xt, xs, hsrc), 0.5 * nt)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h_best = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h_best = make_initial(700)
    h_best = np.nan_to_num(h_best, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = int(len(h_best))
    h_best = project_box_sum(h_best, 0.5 * n)
    c = corr_counts(h_best)
    best_c = c5_from_counts(c, n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 240.0:
        h2, c2 = lp_phase(h_best, n, (3, 5, 10, 20, 40), min(deadline, time.time() + 140.0), 6)
        if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
        h2, c2 = topq_flow(h_best, n, (5, 10, 20, 40), min(deadline, time.time() + 120.0), 16)
        if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        h2, c2 = cd_polish(h_best, n, 2, deadline - 50.0, rng)
        if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    for _ in range(6):
        if time.time() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        h2, c2 = lp_phase(h_best, n, (5, 10, 20), min(deadline - 90.0, time.time() + 35.0), 2)
        if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2
        h2, c2 = cd_polish(h_best, n, 1, min(deadline - 45.0, time.time() + 35.0), rng)
        if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    target = 0.5 * n
    for scale in (0.002, 0.005, 0.010):
        if time.time() > deadline - 140.0:
            break
        hp = project_box_sum(h_best + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), target)
        hp, pc = lp_phase(hp, n, (5, 10), min(deadline - 90.0, time.time() + 35.0), 2)
        hp, pc = topq_flow(hp, n, (5, 10), min(deadline - 65.0, time.time() + 25.0), 6)
        hp, pc = cd_polish(hp, n, 1, min(deadline - 25.0, time.time() + 15.0), rng)
        if pc + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c, n = hp, pc, len(hp)
            target = 0.5 * n

    for nt in (n + 20, n - 20, 760, 680, 620):
        if time.time() > deadline - 80.0:
            break
        nt = int(nt)
        if nt < 200 or nt > 1000 or nt == n:
            continue
        hp = resample_h(h_best, n, nt)
        hp, pc = cd_polish(hp, nt, 1, deadline - 25.0, rng)
        if pc + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c, n = hp, pc, nt

    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        h2, c2 = cd_polish(h_best, n, 1, deadline - 1.0, rng)
        if c2 + 1e-14 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    h_best = project_box_sum(h_best, 0.5 * n)
    c = corr_counts(h_best)
    best_c = c5_from_counts(c, n)
    return h_best, float(best_c), int(n)
```
